## [2026-09-13 17:54] Dashboard tick: keep — Top-10 shipped progress CSV / JSON export (Move #128.z one-click action)

- **Status:** keep
- **Reason:** Completes the cross-page-intelligence + state-persistence pattern with a portable handoff artifact. Every "Mark shipped" click on `/top-10` writes to `ecom-ops:top10-shipped:v1`; this new `<Top10ExportButton>` reads the SAME key and produces a one-row-per-(move × playbook) CSV or JSON download that the operator can paste into a Slack thread, email, or Notion handoff. Closes the operator-progress loop with a portable artifact (ship → track on home → export for the team).
- **Feature class:** one-click-action + state-persistence + cross-page-intelligence (export reads the same localStorage key the `/top-10` per-row toggle and the home-page card write to — operator state, made portable)
- **What shipped:** (a) `dashboard/src/components/top10-export-button.tsx` — NEW (~310 LOC) — `Top10ExportButton` client component with two layout modes: full toolbar (mounted inside `<Top10ShippedProgress>` on `/top-10`) and `compact` button-row (mounted below the Overview card on `/`). Hydrates `ecom-ops:top10-shipped:v1` on mount, subscribes to the canonical `ecom-ops:top10-shipped:update` custom DOM event + the standard `storage` event for cross-tab sync. (b) Builds an export payload by joining the operator's shipped map against `content.top10.status[]` + the canonical `TOP10_PLAYBOOK_MAP` via `resolvePlaybookLinksForMove` — one row per (move × playbook) pair, with `rank`, `move`, `move_id`, `shipped_at`, `playbook_slug`, `playbook_title`, `playbook_href`, `canonical_shipped`, `canonical_pending`. Moves with no direct playbook surface as `(no playbook unlocked)` so the handoff receiver knows the move shipped but didn't unlock a playbook. (c) Filename: `ecom-ops-top10-shipped-<YYYY-MM-DD>.csv` / `.json` — date-stamped for handoff version control. (d) Disabled until hydrated AND the operator has at least one shipped move (no point exporting an empty CSV); title text shows "Export my N shipped moves as CSV / JSON" or "Mark at least one move as shipped on /top-10 to enable export" when empty. (e) `dashboard/app/top-10/page.tsx` — added import + mounted the export button as `<Top10ShippedProgress>` children (the progress card already had a `children` slot reserved for exactly this kind of add-on). (f) `dashboard/app/page.tsx` — added import + mounted the export button in `compact` mode directly below the existing `<Top10ShippedOverviewCard>` so the home-page card has both the visual progress card AND the one-click export.
- **Where to see it:** https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/top-10 — scroll to the Progress card at top, the new "↓ CSV" + "↓ JSON" buttons appear under the twin bars. Mark a couple moves shipped, click CSV, browser downloads `ecom-ops-top10-shipped-2026-09-13.csv` with one row per (move × playbook) you shipped. Same buttons also live on the home page under the "Top 10 — shipped by you" card.
- **Verification:** (a) RED — feature was absent (`grep -c 'top10-export-button' dashboard/src/components/ = 0` before writing). (b) GREEN — `npm run build` SUCCEEDED — `/top-10` route 6.52 kB / 122 kB First Load JS (up from prior 4.86 kB / 120 kB; the ~310 LOC + the row-builder useMemo + the `resolvePlaybookLinksForMove` join add ~1.7 kB to the page bundle, just under the 2 kB diff threshold for a single-feature tick). 172 static pages prerendered, no type errors. (c) `vercel deploy --prod --yes --token "$VERCEL_TOKEN"` SUCCEEDED — `dashboard-30t5o4ttf-mattiasadem-5021s-projects.vercel.app` (deployment-id `dpl_FnVbTp5PGNuJYMrTCbdVjxznfDz3`, `READY`). (d) `vercel alias set` SUCCEEDED in 1s — `https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app now points to dashboard-30t5o4ttf-mattiasadem-5021s-projects.vercel.app` (v2.99.26 canonical-alias rotation). (e) Live curl: `https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/top-10` returned **HTTP 200** with 3 SSR sentinel markers (`top10-export` + `top10-export-csv` + `top10-export-json`). (f) Live curl `https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/_next/static/chunks/app/top-10/page-b2adb980746a1184.js` (21,162 bytes) returned **all 6 sentinels** in the shipped client bundle: `Top10ExportButton` + `ecom-ops-top10-shipped-` + `playbook_slug` + `top10-export` + `top10-export-csv` + `top10-export-json`. (g) Live curl `https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/_next/static/chunks/app/page-7362627880e31c7e.js` (38,846 bytes, the home-page chunk) also returned **all 6 sentinels**, confirming the compact export button on `/` shipped too. (h) `df -h /data` returned 15G free.

- **What shipped:** (a) `dashboard/src/components/ikas-live-card.tsx` — added `REFRESH_INTERVAL_MS = 30_000` constant + `secondsUntilRefresh` state + second `useEffect` keyed on `state.kind` that owns two `setInterval`s (a 1s countdown ticker + a 30s `fetchOverview({ silent: true })` poll, both `clearInterval`'d in cleanup). On `state.kind === "error"` the effect short-circuits and `secondsUntilRefresh` is set to 0 — the polling suspends itself and waits for the operator's manual ↻ Retry to re-arm. `fetchOverview` now takes `{ silent?: boolean }` — non-silent (first mount + manual reload) shows the "Contacting api.myikas.com…" loading placeholder; silent (auto-poll) keeps the previous `live` tiles visible so the numbers don't flicker empty every 30s. (b) Header strip gets a fourth segment with `<span data-testid="ikas-auto-refresh-status" aria-live="polite">` that renders `Next refresh in 17s` (live, foreground tabular-nums) / `Auto-refresh paused` (error, amber-600/dark:amber-400) / `Connecting…` (loading). `aria-live="polite"` so screen readers announce the countdown transitions. (c) ErrorPanel footer note explains why auto-refresh is paused and how to re-arm it (click ↻ Retry → GraphQL succeeds → state returns to `live` → countdown restarts automatically).

## [2026-09-11 23:50] Dashboard tick: keep — `/journal` Real change log (Move #6.x live-data integration + cross-page intelligence)

- **Branch:** dashboard-improver / 2026-09-11
- **Status:** keep
- **Reason:** PASSES improvement metric on 3 axes: (1) **live-data integration** — the new `<GitChangeLog />` panel on `/journal` renders the actual `git log` output for the dashboard subtree, captured at build time by an extended `parseGitLog()` step in `dashboard/scripts/parse-content.mjs`; (2) **cross-page intelligence** — git history is now queryable through the same filter chips + search box + copy-as-markdown pattern as `<JournalSearch />`, so the operator has a single mental model for both views; (3) **state persistence** — filters (query / authors / prefixes) survive reloads via `ecom-ops:git-log:filters:v1` localStorage, so a focused "what changed in the last 50 commits by `hermesagent`?" view stays sticky.
- **Feature class:** live-data-integration + interactive-tool + state-persistence (3-in-1, one bounded tick)
- **What shipped:** (a) `dashboard/scripts/parse-content.mjs` — new `parseGitLog()` that shells out to `git -C <root> log --pretty=format:<HASH>%n<SHORT>%n<DATE>%n<AUTHOR>%n<SUBJECT>%n--END-SUBJECT--%n<BODY>%n--END-COMMIT--> -- dashboard/ docs/journal.md` and parses the result into an array of `{sha, date, author, subject, body}` (capped at 200 commits, graceful failure → `[]` if git unavailable). (b) `dashboard/src/lib/content.ts` — new `GitCommit` interface + `gitCommits: GitCommit[]` field on the `Content` interface + `counts.gitCommits`. (c) `dashboard/src/components/git-change-log.tsx` — 290-line React component with: search input, conventional-commit prefix chips (feat/fix/chore/docs/refactor/perf/style/test/skill/journal), author chips, click-to-expand commit body, GitHub deep links on each SHA, copy-as-markdown for the filtered list, and a "show all N commits / collapse to 30" toggle. (d) `dashboard/app/journal/page.tsx` — wired `<GitChangeLog />` below the existing `<JournalSearch />` with a header paragraph explaining the two views.
- **Why a build-time capture (not a runtime API route):** the Vercel-side build only ships the dashboard subtree — no `.git` directory. A `/api/git-log` server route would always return "no commits". Capturing at build time means every cron tick re-bakes the log via `node scripts/parse-content.mjs`, so the data is always fresh on every deploy.
- **Where to see it:** https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/journal — scroll past the JournalSearch timeline to see "Real change log" panel with 200 commit history.
- **Verification:** (a) `node scripts/parse-content.mjs` SUCCEEDED — wrote `src/lib/content.json` with `gitCommits: 200`. (b) `npm run build` SUCCEEDED — `/journal` route now pre-renders 5.92 kB + 117 kB First Load JS (was 135 B + 103 kB before; the new component ships its own JS chunk). (c) `vercel deploy --prod --yes --token "$VERCEL_TOKEN"` SUCCEEDED — `▲ Production https://dashboard-dffsfqik4-mattiasadem-5021s-projects.vercel.app` (deployment-id `dpl_xHiXXxYVWjTypmDT3FEeBP2pQogW`, `READY`). (d) `vercel alias set dashboard-dffsfqik4-...vercel.app ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app --token "$VERCEL_TOKEN"` SUCCEEDED in 989ms. (e) Live curl: `https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/journal` returned **HTTP 200 / 224,841 bytes** with 4+ sentinel tokens verified: `Real change log` + `commit/c69ea7b` + `hermesagent` + `Move #114 per-cohort-message-engine`. (f) JS bundle grep: `page-9a7f4ee273eb4053.js` contains `Showing` + `conventional` strings confirming the new client-side component shipped.
- **Next action:** natural follow-ups: (a) **Move #6.11 — journal↔git cross-reference** — for each git commit, link it to the closest matching journal entry by date so operators can see "this code change → this journal explanation" bidirectional; (b) **Move #6.12 — per-PR diff preview** — fetch GitHub PR list and show inline diff for the most recent 5 open PRs; (c) **Move #6.13 — build-status badge overlay** — color-code each commit green/red based on whether the Vercel deploy succeeded.
